## 1. Executive Summary & Tech Stack

This platform is a multi-tenant B2B SaaS ERP & HRMS that handles employee master management, biometric/GPS geofenced attendance, multi-tier leave workflows, statutory Indian payroll computation (PF/ESI/PT/TDS), Decentro KYC onboarding, Cashfree SaaS subscriptions, and immutable audit logs.

### Core Stack:
- **Framework & Runtime**: Next.js 15+ (App Router Route Handlers `/api/v1/...`) with Node.js runtime & TypeScript.
- **Database**: PostgreSQL with **Prisma ORM** + native **Postgres Row-Level Security (RLS)**.
- **Identity & Auth**: Multi-tenant JWT (Access + Refresh tokens) + Bcrypt PIN/Password hashing.
- **KYC & Verification**: **Decentro** (DigiLocker/OTP Aadhaar, NSDL PAN, Bank Penny Drop).
- **Billing & Subscriptions**: **Cashfree Payments** (UPI Autopay, Recurring B2B Subscriptions, GST Invoicing).
- **Storage**: **AWS S3** with secure pre-signed upload & download URLs.
- **In-Process Schedulers**: **`node-cron`** (auto-checkout, absence marking, quota resets).
- **Validation**: Strict **Zod** schema parsing on every request body.
- **Testing**: Native Node.js test runner via `tsx --test`.

---

## 2. System Architecture & Multi-Tenancy

The platform serves **four distinct client roles** through a single unified API and database:
1. **Super Admin**: SaaS management, plans, companies, global billing.
2. **Company Owner & HR Admin**: Company settings, branch offices, employees, live attendance, payroll runs, approvals.
3. **Managers**: Department attendance, team leave approvals.
4. **Employees (Web & Future Mobile App)**: Geofenced punch-in/out, leave requests, reimbursement claims, payslips.

```mermaid
graph TD
    Client[Web Admin / Employee Mobile App] -->|API Requests with Bearer JWT| Gateway[Next.js API Gateway /api/v1]
    
    subgraph Multi-Tenant Security Guard
        Gateway --> AuthGuard[Auth & RBAC Middleware]
        AuthGuard --> TenantContext[withTenantContext Wrapper<br/>SET LOCAL app.current_tenant_id]
    end

    subgraph Core Domain Services
        TenantContext --> AuthMod[Auth & RBAC Service]
        TenantContext --> SaaSMod[Super Admin & Billing Service]
        TenantContext --> EmpMod[Employee & Onboarding Service]
        TenantContext --> AttMod[Attendance & Geofencing Service]
        TenantContext --> LeaveMod[Leaves & Approvals Service]
        TenantContext --> PayMod[Payroll & Compliance Service]
        TenantContext --> ExpMod[Reimbursements & Advances Service]
        TenantContext --> CRMMod[CRM & Reports Service]
    end

    subgraph Data & Storage Layer
        CoreDomain[All Domain Services] --> PostgresRLS[PostgreSQL RDS with RLS Policies]
        CoreDomain --> S3[AWS S3 Pre-Signed Storage]
    end
```

### Connection-Pool-Safe Postgres Row-Level Security (RLS)
To prevent cross-tenant data leaks when connection poolers (PgBouncer, Prisma pool) reuse database connections across requests:
- Every query is wrapped inside `withTenantContext(companyId, async (tx) => { ... })`.
- The wrapper executes `SET LOCAL app.current_tenant_id = '<companyId>'` within a dedicated transaction.
- Because `SET LOCAL` is transaction-scoped, it automatically resets on commit/rollback.
- **Fail-Closed Safeguard**: A fresh pooled connection without tenant context evaluates to `false` in RLS policies, returning **zero rows**.


### Core Entities Summary:
- `SuperAdmin`: Master platform administrator accounts.
- `Company`: Multi-tenant root entity (`companyCode` e.g. `ACME-8X2F`).
- `User`: Identity entity supporting Email/Password (Admins) and Mobile Phone + 4/6-digit PIN (Employees).
- `Branch`: Office branches with GPS coordinates (`latitude`, `longitude`, `radiusMeters`).
- `Employee`: Full employment profile with manager hierarchy (`reportingManagerId`).
- `EmployeeKYC`: Decentro verification records (`aadhaarNumber`, `aadhaarLast4`, `panNumber`, `bankAccountNumber`, `bankAccountLast4`, `ifscCode`, `decentroTxnId`).
- `AttendanceRecord` & `AttendancePunch`: Daily aggregates and granular punch logs with GPS and mock detection.
- `LeaveType`, `LeaveBalance`, `LeaveRequest`: Configurable company leave rules with multi-tier approval states.
- `SalaryStructure`, `PayrollRun`, `PayrollItem`: Monthly gross-to-net calculations and payslip metadata.
- `ReimbursementClaim` & `SalaryAdvance`: Expense claims with S3 receipts and loans with automatic EMI deductions.
- `CRMLead`, `CRMCustomer`, `CRMActivity`: Lightweight CRM pipeline.
- `AuditLog`: System audit trail with database-enforced immutability (`REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE`).
- `WebhookEvent`: Idempotency ledger for Decentro & Cashfree webhook deliveries.

---

## 4. Authentication, RBAC & Security

### Role Enum:
- `SUPER_ADMIN`: Access to SaaS management, platform companies, and global billing.
- `COMPANY_OWNER`: Full administrative access to their company's ERP.
- `HR_ADMIN`: Employee onboarding, attendance regularization, and payroll processing.
- `MANAGER`: Team attendance viewing and direct report leave/expense approvals.
- `EMPLOYEE`: Personal punch-in/out, leave applications, reimbursement submissions, payslips.

### Dual Auth Strategies:
1. **Admin / Owner Web App Login**:
   - `POST /api/v1/auth/login` (Email + Password)
2. **Employee Mobile / Punch Login**:
   - `POST /api/v1/auth/employee-login` (Mobile Phone + 4/6-digit PIN)
3. **Company Registration**:
   - `POST /api/v1/auth/register` (Auto-provisions Company, HQ Branch, Admin User, Shift, Leave Types, and 14-day trial).

---

## 5. External Provider Adapter Pattern

All external third-party dependencies are decoupled behind strict TypeScript interfaces in `src/providers/`. The active provider is chosen at runtime via environment variables without scattering `if (isDev)` checks throughout business logic.

### Mock Provider Test Fixtures:
- **Aadhaar**: Number ending in `0000` triggers simulated UIDAI checksum failure.
- **PAN**: Number ending in `X` triggers simulated NSDL not found.
- **Bank Account**: Number ending in `0000` triggers simulated Penny Drop rejection.
- **SMS / OTP**: Logs OTP to console/database without blocking on DLT registration.

---

## 6. Core Business Modules & Logic

### 1. Attendance & Geofencing Engine
- **Office Geofencing**: Uses the Haversine spherical formula to calculate the exact distance between the employee's GPS coordinates and their assigned branch office coordinates.
- **Anti-Spam Debounce**: Enforces a 5-second memory debounce per employee to prevent accidental double-taps.
- **Mock GPS Detection**: Detects and rejects simulated location providers.
- **Shift Calculation**: Evaluates late arrival against shift grace periods (e.g. 15 mins) and marks `PRESENT`, `LATE`, or `HALF_DAY`.

### 2. Statutory Payroll Calculation Engine
Formula executed on monthly payroll runs (`POST /api/v1/payroll/runs`):
$$\text{Gross Earnings} = \text{Prorated Basic} + \text{Prorated HRA} + \text{Prorated Allowances}$$
$$\text{Total Deductions} = \text{PF (12\%)} + \text{ESI (0.75\%)} + \text{PT (ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¦ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¹200)} + \text{TDS} + \text{Advance EMI}$$
$$\text{Net Salary Payable} = \text{Gross Earnings} - \text{Total Deductions} + \text{Approved Reimbursements}$$

### 3. Decentro KYC Onboarding
- Full number is stored securely for HR review, with `aadhaarLast4` and `bankAccountLast4` provided for masked UI display.
- Verification status tracks `NOT_SUBMITTED`, `PENDING`, `VERIFIED`, `FAILED`.

---

## 7. API Reference Directory

### Auth Routes
- `POST /api/v1/auth/register`: Register company + owner with 14-day trial.
- `POST /api/v1/auth/login`: Admin/HR login with email & password.
- `POST /api/v1/auth/employee-login`: Employee login with phone & PIN.
- `POST /api/v1/auth/superadmin-login`: Platform superadmin login.
- `POST /api/v1/auth/refresh`: Refresh expired access token.
- `GET /api/v1/auth/me`: Authenticated profile details.

### Organization & Employee Routes
- `GET/POST /api/v1/branches`: Branch office locations with geofencing.
- `GET/POST /api/v1/departments`: Department CRUD.
- `GET/POST /api/v1/shifts`: Shift timings, grace periods, and cutoffs.
- `GET/POST /api/v1/employees`: Employee onboarding and profile search.
- `POST /api/v1/verification/aadhaar`: Decentro Aadhaar verification.
- `POST /api/v1/verification/pan`: Decentro NSDL PAN verification.
- `POST /api/v1/verification/bank`: Decentro bank account penny drop.
- `POST /api/v1/webhooks/decentro`: Decentro webhook listener.

### Attendance & Leaves Routes
- `POST /api/v1/attendance/punch`: Record punch (IN, OUT, BREAK_START, BREAK_END).
- `GET /api/v1/attendance/live`: Live dashboard of current employee statuses.
- `GET /api/v1/attendance/daily`: Monthly attendance calendar logs.
- `GET /api/v1/leaves/types`: Company leave policies.
- `POST /api/v1/leaves/apply`: Submit leave application.

### Payroll, Expenses & Billing Routes
- `POST /api/v1/payroll/structures`: Define salary templates.
- `POST /api/v1/payroll/runs`: Run automated monthly payroll.
- `POST /api/v1/expenses`: Submit reimbursement claim.
- `POST /api/v1/advances`: Request salary advance / loan.
- `POST /api/v1/webhooks/cashfree`: Cashfree subscription & invoice webhook listener.

### CRM & Reports Routes
- `GET/POST /api/v1/crm/leads`: Lead tracking pipeline.
- `GET/POST /api/v1/crm/customers`: Customer list.
- `GET /api/v1/reports/attendance`: Monthly attendance register report.
- `GET /api/v1/reports/payroll`: Monthly payroll register report.

---

## 8. Local Development, Database Setup & Testing

### 1. Prerequisites
- Node.js `v20+` or `v22+`
- PostgreSQL instance (local or cloud: AWS RDS / Supabase / Neon)

### 2. Setup Steps
```bash
# 1. Install dependencies
npm install

# 2. Configure environment
cp .env.example .env

# 3. Generate Prisma client
npx prisma generate

# 4. Push schema to database
npx prisma db push

# 5. Apply Postgres RLS policies (in psql or database console)
# Run contents of: prisma/rls/rls_policies.sql

# 6. Run automated test suite
npm test

# 7. Start local development server
npm run dev
```

---

## 9. Deployment Architecture (AWS App Runner)

The backend is packaged as a standard containerized Node.js application ready for **AWS App Runner**:
- **AWS App Runner**: Automatic HTTPS termination, load balancing, zero server maintenance, auto-scaling on request spikes.
- **AWS RDS PostgreSQL**: Multi-AZ PostgreSQL instance with RLS policies enabled.
- **AWS S3**: Bucket configured with private access, documents retrieved strictly via pre-signed URLs.
